# It is done!
########################################
event = {
	id = 2002004
	random = no
	country = VIC
	style = 2
	picture = "Petain"
	save_date = yes

	trigger = {
		war = { country = GER } 
		lost_national = { country = VIC value = 5 } 
		control = { province = 56 data = -2 } #paris lost
		OR = { 
			region = { region = "France" country = -2 data = 1 value = 50 } 
			#Army destroyed
			NOT = { land_percentage = { country = GER value = 0.4 } }
			#Significant army group encircled at the maginot line
			AND = { 
				lost_national = { country = VIC value = 20 } 
				control = { province = 3172	data = -2 } 
				control = { province = 841	data = -2 } 
				control = { province = 317	data = -2 } 
				control = { province = 1643	data = -2 } 
				control = { province = 73	data = -3 } 
				garrison = { province = 73 	country = -3 type = land size = 25 area = yes }
			}
        }
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 6
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	name = "4050name"
	desc = "4050desc"

	action = {
		name = "evt_4050a"
		command = { trigger = { control = { province = 59 data = VIC } } type = capital which = 59 } # Bordeaux - France +
		#command = { type = dissent value = 10 }
		command = { type = trigger which = 2003001 }
	} 
}

event = {
	id = 2003001
	random = no
	country = GER
	style = 2

	name = "4052name" # France enquires about the conditions of armistice
	desc = "4053desc" # After the loss of Paris, the British offer an union to the French government. Before delivering his answer, he like to enquire about the conditions of armistice demanded by Germany. If you demand merciful conditions of armistice, the Frenchmen will be more disposed to collaborate and to refuse British approaches. If contrariwise you demand very hard conditions of armistice, they will have tendency to get closer to Britain. History is yours to make..
	picture = "Petain"

	action = {
        ai_chance = 100
		name = "4053b" # Let us claim a historical partition
		command = { type = setflag which = Rethondes2 } # North coast + Paris + Loire + Atlantic Coast
    } 
	action = {
        ai_chance = 0
		name = "4053a" # Let us claim Paris and the north
		command = { type = setflag which = Rethondes1 } # North coast + Paris
		command = { type = dissent value = 2 } #a bit too lenient
    } 
}

event = {
	id = 2002006
	random = no
	country = VIC
	style = 2

	name = "4054name" # French decision
	desc = "France must now decide whether to accept the German terms or fight on."
	picture = "Petain"

	trigger = { 
		event = 2003001 # France enquires about the conditions of armistice
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 3
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = { 
        trigger = { flag = Rethondes1 }
        ai_chance = 100
		name = "Sign the armistice"
		command = { type = secederegion which = -1 value = "France" when = 1 } 
		command = { type = dissent value = 5 }
		
		command = { type = leave_alliance when = 1 }
		command = { type = peace which = GER value = 1 }
		command = { type = access which = GER }
		command = { trigger = { alliance = { country = GER country = ITA } } type = access which = ITA }
		command = { trigger = { alliance = { country = GER country = SPA } } type = access which = SPA }
		
	    command = { type = secederegion which = GER value = "Benelux" when = 1 }
	    command = { type = secederegion which = GER value = "Germany" when = 1 }	
		
		command = { type = event which = 2400009 where = VIC when = 0 } #frontLeader no
		command = { type = event which = 2401502 where = VIC when = 0 } #noExpGermany
		command = { type = event which = 2002007 where = GER when = 2 } #The armistice of Rethondes
    } 
	action = {
        trigger = { flag = Rethondes1 }
        ai_chance = 0
		name = "4054c" # France fights on
		command = { type = dissent value = 35 }
		command = { type = capital which = 949 } # Alger - Algeria
        command = { type = secedeprovince which = GER value = 72 when = 2 } # Strasbourg - France
        command = { type = secedeprovince which = GER value = 73 when = 2 } # Metz - France
        command = { type = secedeprovince which = GER value = 316 when = 2 } # Colmar - France
		command = { type = trigger which = 2002020 } #French Fleet Seized
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 132 } #Courbet - Battleship 3
        command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 133 } #Paris - Battleship 3
        command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 136 } #Pluton - Light Cruiser 4
        command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 137 } #Jeanne d'Arc - Light Cruiser 4
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 139 } #4ème Flottille de Contre-Torpilleurs - Destroyer 1
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 138 } #5ème Flottille de Contre-Torpilleurs - Destroyer 1
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 150 } #1ère Flottille de Sous-Marins - Submarine 6
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 151 } #2ème Flottille de Sous-Marins - Submarine 6
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 120 } #1ère Flottille de Convoi - Transport 1
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 115 } #2ème Flottille de Convoi - Transport 2
		
		command = { type = clrflag which = Rethondes1 }
    }
	action = {
        trigger = { flag = Rethondes2 }
        ai_chance = 99
		name = "Sign the armistice"
		command = { type = secederegion which = -1 value = "France" when = 1 } 
		command = { type = dissent value = 10 }
		
		command = { type = leave_alliance when = 1 }
		command = { type = peace which = GER value = 1 }
		command = { type = access which = GER }
		command = { trigger = { alliance = { country = GER country = ITA } } type = access which = ITA }
		command = { trigger = { alliance = { country = GER country = SPA } } type = access which = SPA }
		
	    command = { type = secederegion which = GER value = "Benelux" when = 1 }
	    command = { type = secederegion which = GER value = "Germany" when = 1 }	
		
		
		command = { type = event which = 2400009 where = VIC when = 0 } #frontLeader no
		command = { type = event which = 2401502 where = VIC when = 0 } #noExpGermany
		command = { type = event which = 2002007 where = GER when = 2 } #The armistice of Rethondes
    } 
	action = {
        trigger = { flag = Rethondes2 }
        ai_chance = 1
		name = "4054c" # France fights on
		command = { type = dissent value = 30 }
		command = { type = capital which = 949 } # Alger - Algeria +
        command = { type = secedeprovince which = GER value = 72 when = 2 } # Strasbourg - France
        command = { type = secedeprovince which = GER value = 73 when = 2 } # Metz - France
        command = { type = secedeprovince which = GER value = 316 when = 2 } # Colmar - France
		command = { type = trigger which = 2002020 } #French Fleet Seized
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 132 } #Courbet - Battleship 3
        command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 133 } #Paris - Battleship 3
        command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 136 } #Pluton - Light Cruiser 4
        command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 137 } #Jeanne d'Arc - Light Cruiser 4
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 139 } #4ème Flottille de Contre-Torpilleurs - Destroyer 1
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 138 } #5ème Flottille de Contre-Torpilleurs - Destroyer 1
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 150 } #1ère Flottille de Sous-Marins - Submarine 6
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 151 } #2ème Flottille de Sous-Marins - Submarine 6

		command = { type = clrflag which = Rethondes2 }
    }

event = {
	id = 2002007
	random = no
	country = GER
	style = 2

		name = ACTION_NAME_OK
		command = { type = dissent value = -10 }

event = {
	id = 2002020
	random = no
	country = ITA
	style = 2

	name = "EVT_2006022_NAME" #French Fleet Seized
	desc = "EVT_2006022_DESC" 

	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_GREAT" #Great
		command = { type = dissent value = -1 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "Courbet" value = battleship when = 2 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "Paris" value = battleship when = 2 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "Pluton" value = light_cruiser when = 3 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "Jeanne d'Arc" value = light_cruiser when = 3 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "4ème Flottille de Contre-Torpilleurs" value = destroyer when = 0 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "5ème Flottille de Contre-Torpilleurs" value = destroyer when = 0 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "1ère Flottille de Sous-Marins" value = submarine when = 5 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "2ème Flottille de Sous-Marins" value = submarine when = 5 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "1ère Flottille de Convoi" value = transport when = 0 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "2ème Flottille de Convoi" value = transport when = 0 }
    } 
}

event = {
	id = 2002021
	random = no
	country = ENG
	style = 2

	name = "Fate of the Channel Islands"
	desc = "After the defeat in the Battle of France, we need to decide whether or not to defend the Channel Islands. Historically, the channel islands were seen as of no strategic importance and were given up without a shot. If we choose to defend the islands, we will need to have at least 3 divisions stationed on the islands within a month." 

	action = {
		name = "Evacuate the islands"
		ai_chance = 100
		trigger = {
			exists = U56
			NOT = { war = { country = ENG country = U56 } }
			NOT = { war = { country = GER country = U56 } }
		}
		command = { type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { control = { province = 7131 data = -3 } } type = secedeprovince which = U56 value = 7131 				when = 2 } 
		command = { trigger = { control = { province = 1720 data = -3 } } type = secedeprovince which = U56 value = 1720 				when = 2 } 
		
		command = { type = event which = 2002022 where = GER when = 1 }
    } 
	action = {
		name = "Commit to their defense"
		ai_chance = 0
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				control = { province = 7131 data = -3 }
				control = { province = 1720 data = -3 }
			}
		}
		command = { type = event which = 2002023 where = ENG when = 30 }
    } 
}

event = {
	id = 2002022
	random = no
	country = GER
	style = 2

	name = "Fate of the Channel Islands"
	desc = "After the Allied defeat in the Battle of France, the British government decided that the Channel Islands were of no strategic importance and would not be defended. Despite the reluctance of Prime Minister Winston Churchill, the British government gave up the oldest possession of the Crown 'without firing a single shot.' The Channel Islands served no purpose for the Germans other than the propaganda value of occupying British territory. The decision to abandon the islands was at first kept secret from Germany to make sure that evacuation is not disrupted, and when it was complete, London officially declared the islands an 'open city' and the German Luftwaffe landed unopposed." 

	action = {
		name = "Victory after victory!"
		command = { type = dissent value = -0.5 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = GER value = 7131 	when = 2 } 
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = GER value = 1720 	when = 2 } 
    } 
}

event = {
	id = 2002023
	random = no
	country = ENG
	style = 2

	name = "Fate of the Channel Islands"
	desc = "Despite assuring the Islanders and the British public that the islands would be defended, the London government still withdrew all defense from the islands to everybody's surprise. While all is fair in love and war, the Parliament and the British people were unpleasantly suprised to learn that the Germans landed on the islands unopposed." 

	action = {
		name = "It had to be done"
		trigger = {
			NOT = { garrison = { country = -3 province = 7131 type = land size = 3 area = 3 } }
			NOT = { garrison = { country = -3 province = 1720 type = land size = 3 area = 3 } }
		}
		command = { type = dissent value = 2 }
    } 
}

event = {
	id = 2002035
	random = no
	country = VIC
	style = 2

	name = "Fate of Paris"
	desc = "As the German forces rapidly advanced towards Paris, the French government needed to decide on the fate of the city. Paris held immense cultural, historical, and symbolic value, and its ruin would have been a catastrophic loss for France. Prime Minister Paul Reynaud recognized that defending Paris would lead to heavy bombing, street fighting, and severe civilian casualties, and given the state of the French Army and disarray in the Allied camp, the military leadership was deeply unconvinced that Paris could be held in the end. As such, Reynaud made the decision to declare Paris an open city and withdraw forces southward to avoid encirclement."
	picture = "Petain"

	trigger = { 
		war = { country = GER }
		alliance = { country = ENG }
		NOT = { event = 2002008 }
		OR = {
			control = { province = 7088	data = -2 }
			control = { province = 42	data = -2 }
			control = { province = 3176	data = -2 }
			control = { province = 55	data = -2 }
			control = { province = 63	data = -2 }
			control = { province = 3175	data = -2 }
			control = { province = 7089	data = -2 }
			control = { province = 54	data = -2 }
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 3
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = { 
		name = "Paris - open city"
        ai_chance = 99
		command = { type = dissent value = 1 }
		command = { type = urban_attack 		which = land value = -70  }
		command = { type = urban_defense 		which = land value = -110 }
		command = { type = event which = 2002036 where = VIC when = 60 }
    } 
	action = {
        ai_chance = 1
		name = "We will fight for Paris"
		command = { type = dissent value = 5 }
		command = { type = money 										value = -2000 }
		command = { type = construct which = ic 			where = 56	value = -2    }
		command = { type = construct which = infrastructure where = 56	value = -20   }
    } 
}

# L'Etat Français
##########################

event = {
	id = 2002008
	random = no
	country = VIC
	style = 2
	save_date = yes

	name = "4057name" # L'Etat Français
	desc = "4057desc" # Field marshal Pétain uses the French defeat to take power. It flings a final blow to the Republic and sets up a strict regime, the French State. The emergence of such regime opens new geo-strategical opportunities for Germany.
	picture = "Petain"

	action = {
		name = "2002008text"
        #move capital to Vichy
        command = { type = capital which = 360 } # Vichy - France +
		#Disaster... There is some kind of hardcoded bullshit which causes expeditionary AI to become completely retarded if VIC is a puppet of GER but not allied. It works fine if VIC is changed to any other tag though.
		#Possible solutions... 
		#1. Change VIC to VIC. Have to rewrite all the events, and "type=country" will crash in MP so need to have some alternative which is even less than ideal.
		#2. Drop the whole "puppet" thing entirely. May be the best option for the forseable future, but it will look a bit weird that Petain is not a puppet, but De Gaulle is.
		#3. Make VIC both puppet and allied, BUT write an event which regularly checks VIC territory for the presense of Axis troops/navy/air. Very performance heavy and also not foolproof. Subs can stay in ports only briefly when rebasing for example.
		#4. Completely replace VIC with another tag from the very beginning?
		#command = { type = country 		which = VIC   } 
		command = { type = local_setflag 	which = Vichy }
		
		#At the beginning of 1942, the numbers of the Armistice Army were at 550,000 men, including 21,000 officers.
		#In addition, the size of the Gendarmerie was fixed at 60,000 men plus an anti-aircraft force of 10,000 men.
		#However, these numbers include the Levant Army and Indochina army. Approximate distribution
		#Metropolitain France: 160,000 men (100k army, 60k militia)
		#North Africa: 143,000 men (127k army, 16k militia)
		#West Africa: 40,000 men (mostly militia)
		#Levant: 45,000 men (15k army, 30k militia)
		#Indochina: 60,000 men (20k army, 40k militia)
		#Madagascar: 10,000 men (militia)
		command = { type = demobilize which = armor 			when = 0 where = 0 value = 2  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = light_armor 		when = 0 where = 0 value = 2  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = garrison	 		when = 0 where = 0 value = 3  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = hq		 		when = 0 where = 0 value = 3  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = cavalry	 		when = 0 where = 0 value = 3  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = motorized 		when = 0 where = 0 value = 5  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = bergsjaeger 		when = 0 where = 0 value = 5  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = infantry	 		when = 0 where = 0 value = 25 }
		
		command = { trigger = { army = { size = 50 } } type = demobilize which = land when = 0 where = 0 value = 50 } #leave ~50 land units (best models)
		
		#French had only 612 combat planes left at the end of the campaign, which germany reluctantly allowed them to keep
		command = { type = demobilize which = strategic_bomber	when = 0 where = 0 value = 0  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = tactical_bomber	when = 0 where = 0 value = 5  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = air	 			when = 0 where = 0 value = 10 }
		
		command = { type = local_setflag which = FRA_AIR when = 1 value = 5 }
		command = { type = event which = 975 where = GER when = 0 }
		command = { type = event which = 975 where = GER when = 0 }
		command = { type = event which = 975 where = GER when = 0 }

	id = 2002016
	random = no
	#invisible
	#country = VIC
	persistent = yes
	style = 2

	trigger = { 
		country = VIC
		event = 2002008
		NOT = { alliance = { country = GER } }
		OR = {
			access = { country = VIC country = GER }
			access = { country = VIC country = ITA }
			access = { country = VIC country = SPA }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { war = { country = ENG } }
			non_aggression = { country = VIC country = ENG }
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	name = "French Neutrality"
	desc = "By the terms of the Second Armistice at Compiegne, France was to remain strictly neutral while Germany was allowed to occupy the northern and western areas of metropolitain France until the end of war with Britain. While the Luftwaffe pilots occasionally overflew Vichy French territory, and there is some evidence that German and Italian subs may have temporarily entered French ports in the Mediterranean for resupply, any major or permanent Axis military presense in Vichy territory would constitute a major breach of the armistice terms and ruin any pretense of continued neutrality."
	picture = "Petain"

	action = { 
		name = "No Germans or dogs allowed!"
		command = { type = end_access which = GER }	
		command = { type = end_access which = ITA }	
		command = { type = end_access which = SPA }	
	} 
}

		NOT = { 
			exists = U01 
			region = { region = France country = -3 data = 2 value = 1 } 
		}
		war =  { country = ENG country = GER }
	}
	decision_trigger = { 
		event = { id = 2002008 days = 10 } #L'Etat Français
		NOT = { exists = U01 }
		war =  { country = ENG country = GER }
	}
	trigger = { 
		event = { id = 2002008 days = 10 } #L'Etat Français
		NOT = {
			exists = U01 
			region = { region = France country = -3 data = 2 value = 1 }

event = {
	id = 950
	random = no
	country = VIC
	style = 2
	picture = "Mers-el-Kebir"
	decision_picture = "decision_grand_offensive4"

	decision = {
		war = { country = ENG }
		NOT = { war = { country = GER } }
		#non_aggression = { country = VIC country = ENG } 
		NOT = { alliance = { country = GER } }
	}
	decision_trigger = { 
		event = { id = 2002011 days = 30 } #Attack on Mers-el-Kebir
		supplies = 500
	}
	trigger = {
		random = 30
		war = { country = ENG }
		NOT = { war = { country = GER } }
		#non_aggression = { country = VIC country = ENG } 
		NOT = { alliance = { country = GER } }
		event = { id = 2002011 days = 30 } #Attack on Mers-el-Kebir
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	name = "Battle of Dakar and French reprisals"
	desc = "After the British attack on the French Fleet at Mers-el-Kebir, the Vichy government authorized bombing raids of Gibraltar as a response. The first attack was meant as more of a warning, and the majority of the bombs were deliberately dropped short of their target. But the raids continued, especially once the British attempted to help the rogue general De Gaulle wrestle control over the French colony of Senegal. The battle of Dakar was a failure for the Allies, with several British ships receiving heavy damage, and further Vichy raids on Gibraltar followed suit. 64 French bombers took off from Morocco and dropped 150 bombs on Gibraltar, dealing heavy damage to the fort and naval base, as well as several auxillary ships. The next day, the French returned with another bombing raid. However, as the Allies withdrew from Dakar, the French ceased their bombing raids and the tensions cooled once again."

	action = {
		name = "Authorize reprisals!"
		command = { type = supplies value = -500 }
		command = { type = trigger which = 951 }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 960
	random = no
	tag = { ENG USA }
	style = 2
	picture = "Petain"
	decision_picture = "decision_grand_offensive4"

	decision = {
		war = { country = VIC }
		war = { country = GER }
		NOT = { war = { country = GER country = VIC } }
		#NOT = { alliance = { country = GER country = VIC } }
		#non_aggression = { country = VIC country = ENG }
	}
	decision_trigger = { 
		#OR = { 
		#	control = { province = 348	data = -3 }
		#	control = { province = 932	data = -3 }
		#	control = { province = 31	data = -3 }
		#}
	}
	trigger = {
		war = { country = VIC }
		war = { country = GER }
		NOT = { war = { country = GER country = VIC } }
		#NOT = { alliance = { country = GER country = VIC } }
		#non_aggression = { country = VIC country = ENG }
		#NOT = { region = { region = "Egypt" country = -2 value = 5 } }
		
		OR = { 
			control = { province = 348	data = -3 }
			control = { province = 932	data = -3 }
			control = { province = 31	data = -3 }
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				alliance = { country = ENG country = USA }
				NOT = { flag = debuff_USA }
			}
			event = { id = 2002011 days = 1095 } #3 years since attack on Mers-el-Kebir - UK goes alone
		}
		OR = {
			region = { region = "Libya" country = -3 value = 20 } #Advancing in Libya
			control = { province = 515 data = -3 } #Took Rome
			AND = {
				event = { id = 2002011 days = 1095 } #3 years since attack on Mers-el-Kebir
				alliance = { country = ENG country = USA }
				NOT = { flag = debuff_USA }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				ai = VIC
				NOT = { alliance = { country = GER country = VIC } }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 4392		type = land size = 6  area = 1 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 4392		type = land size = 2  area = 0 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 2911		type = land size = 6  area = 0 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 2911		type = land size = 3  area = 0 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 949		type = land size = 6  area = 0 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 949		type = land size = 3  area = 0 } }
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	name = "Operation Torch"
	desc = "The Allies planned an Anglo-American invasion of French North Africa, the territories of Morocco, Algeria and Tunisia, nominally in the hands of the Vichy French government. With British forces advancing from Egypt, this would eventually allow the Allies to carry out a pincer operation against Axis forces in North Africa. The colonies were held by Vichy France, but the loyalties of the population as well as the French military were mixed. Reports indicated that they might support the Allies."

	action = {
		name = "Begin the operation!"
		command = { trigger = { non_aggression = { country = VIC country = ENG } } type = dissent value = 1 } #Not the most popular target... (still better than getting +5 from breaking the NAP manually)
		command = { type = trigger which = 961 }
		
		command = { trigger = { alliance = { country = ENG country = USA } ai = VIC ai = USA ai = ENG } type = event which = 2405030 where = USA when = 0 }
	}
}

####################################
# Armistice army limits
####################################

event = {
	id = 970
	random = no
	country = VIC
	style = 2
	picture = "Petain"
	persistent = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		NOT = { local_flag = armisticeCheck }
		event = { id = 2002008 days = 90  }
		NOT = { event = 984 } #liberation of france
		NOT = { event = 2003100 } #end of war
		puppet = { country = VIC country = GER }
		OR = {
			army = 61 #potentially over 800,000 men
			garrison = { country = VIC province = 360 type = land size = 21 area = 3 }
			NOT = { garrison = { country = VIC province = 360 type = land size = 10 area = 3 } }
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	name = "Armistice Army limits exceeded"
	desc = "Article IV of the Armistice of 22 June 1940 placed strict limits on the size of the remaining French Army and its deployment area. Our total army size cannot exceed 60 divisions, out of which at most 20 can be located in Metropolitain France at any given time. At the same time, we cannot have less than 10 divisions in Metropolitain France, or else Germany will not trust us to defend against a possible Allied invasion or even maintain order against resistance from the Maquis. We have 15 days to fix the problem, or we will face consequences..."

	action = {
		name = ACTION_NAME_OK
		command = { type = local_setflag which = armisticeCheck  }
		command = { type = set_relation which = GER value = -30 }
		command = { type = event which = 971 where = -1 when = 15 }
		command = { type = event which = 972 where = -1 when = 15 }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 971
	random = no
	country = VIC
	style = 2
	picture = "Petain"
	persistent = yes

	name = "Armistice Army limits exceeded"
	desc = "Article IV of the Armistice of 22 June 1940 placed strict limits on the size of the remaining French Army and its deployment area. Although we briefly went beyond the established limits, we were able to correct the situation quickly without provoking the ire of Germany."

	action = {
		name = ACTION_NAME_GREAT
		trigger = { 
			NOT = {
				army = 61 #potentially over 800,000 men
				garrison = { country = VIC province = 360 type = land size = 21 area = 3 }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = VIC province = 360 type = land size = 10 area = 3 } }
			}
		}
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = armisticeCheck  }
		command = { type = set_relation which = GER value = 20 }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 972
	random = no
	country = VIC
	style = 2
	picture = "Petain"
	persistent = yes

	name = "Armistice Army limits exceeded"
	desc = "Article IV of the Armistice of 22 June 1940 placed strict limits on the size of the remaining French Army and its deployment area. Our failure to bring the army within specified limits has not gone unnoticed"

		name = ACTION_NAME_DAMN
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				army = 61 #potentially over 800,000 men
				garrison = { country = VIC province = 360 type = land size = 21 area = 3 }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = VIC province = 360 type = land size = 10 area = 3 } }
			}
		}
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = armisticeCheck  }
		#command = { type = end_puppet }
		command = { type = set_relation which = GER value = -100 }
		command = { type = event which = 2002049 where = VIC when = 0 } #Vichy coup (chance of failure 15%, will happen though again if the problem is not resolved)
	}
}

####################################
# French Airforce Seized
# 1940: 800-1000 possibly captured in various condition, limiting to 500
# 1942: 300 confiscated?
####################################

event = {
	id = 975
	random = no
	country = GER
	style = 2
	picture = "Petain"
	persistent = yes

	name = "Fate of the French Airforce"
	desc = "During both the fall of France in 1940 as well as after Case Anton, Germany was able to seize a number of French combat aircraft, some of it in servicable condition. While much of the aircraft was outdated, failed German standartization requirements, needed meaintenance and crew training, some of it could still be useful - particularly the Dewoitine D.520 fighter planes. Historically, Germany did not make much combat use of the seized aircraft, preferring to use it for spare parts, for logisitical and for training purposes. But many planes were also sold to other Axis powers like Finland and Bulgaria at low prices, where they saw direct combat service. (Each instance of this event covers approximately 100 appropriated aircraft)"

	action = {
		name = "Put them in service"
		ai_chance = 5
		command = { type = dissent 		value = 0.05  } #Germans are convinced their equipment is superior, why are they being forced to fly foreign planes?
		command = { type = manpowerpool	value = -1    }
		command = { type = supplies		value = -2000 } #crew training & maintenance (2000/5000 = ~40%)
		command = { type = add_division	value = multi_role when = 5	where = -30 } #-30 To simulate difficulty with parts replacement
		command = { type = local_setflag which = FRA_AIR when = 1 value = -1 }
	}
	action = {
		name = "Use for training and parts"
		ai_chance = 40
		#about half the cost of 1 modern mult_role, though German airforce is even cheaper thanks to Goring, so this is a really attractive option
		command = { type = supplies		value = 2500  }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = FRA_AIR when = 1 value = -1 }
	}
	action = {
		name = "Sell to Italy"
		ai_chance = 15
		trigger = { exists = ITA }
		command = { type = money		value = 500 }
		command = { type = relation 	value = 20	which = ITA  }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = FRA_AIR when = 1 value = -1 }
		command = { type = event which = 976 where = ITA when = 0 }
	}
	action = {
		name = "Sell to Spain"
		ai_chance = 1
		trigger = { exists = SPA }

While much of the aircraft was outdated, failed German standartization requirements, needed meaintenance and crew training, some of it could still be useful - particularly the Dewoitine D.520 fighter planes. Historically, Germany did not make much combat use of the seized aircraft, preferring to use it for spare parts, for logisitical and for training purposes. 	country = VIC

		event = 2002008 # French State
		alliance = { country = ENG }
		war = { country = JAP }
		region = { region = France country = -3 value = 60 }
		control = { province = 56 data = -3 } #Paris +
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }
	
	name = "Operation Bright Moon"
	desc = "With the fall of Vichy government and the liberation of France from German occupation, the French colony of Indochina secretely pledged its allegiance to the new government. The Provisional Government sent General Eugene Mordant to prepare a rebellion against the Japanese in Indochina. Mordant, however, was careless – he talked so much that the Japanese Kempeitai swiftly uncovered the plot against them and prepared a preemptive strike against the French."
	style = 2
	
	action = {
		name = "Curses!"
		command = { type = event which = 991 where = U03 when = 1 }
	}
}

event = { 
	id = 2002080
	random = no
	country = GER

	decision = {
		war = { country = ENG }
		exists = VIC
		exists = U04
		NOT = { war = { country = VIC } }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = VIC } }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = U04 } }
		event = { id = 2002008 days = 90 } 
	}
	trigger = {
		war = { country = ENG }
		exists = VIC
		exists = U04
		NOT = { war = { country = VIC } }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = VIC } }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = U04 } }
		OR = {
			#event = { id = 2002008 days = 720 } 
			war = { country = ENG country = IRQ }
			AND = {
				NOT = { non_aggression = { country = U04 country = ENG } }
				war = { country = U04 country = ENG }
			}
			region = { region = "Anatolia"	country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
			area = { 	area = "Palestine"	country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
			area = { 	area = "Iraq"		country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 3
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }
	
	name = "German Access to Syria"
	desc = "On 28 May 1941, Admiral Darlan, on behalf of Vichy France, signed the Paris Protocols, an agreement which granted Germany access to military facilities in Vichy-controlled Syria. The protocols remained unratified, but Charles Huntziger, the Vichy Minister of War, sent orders to allow German and Italian aircraft to refuel in Syria. Marked as Iraqi aircraft, Axis aircraft landed in Syria en route to the Kingdom of Iraq during the Anglo-Iraqi War."
	picture = "Petain"
	style = 2
	
	action = {
		name = "Demand access to Syria"
		command = { type = money value = -100 }
		command = { type = supplies value = -1000 }
		command = { type = event which = 2002081 where = VIC when = 1 }
	}
}

event = { 
	id = 2002083
	random = no
	country = ENG

		event = 2002008
		war = { country = GER }
		war = { country = VIC }
		OR = {
			NOT = { war = { country = U04 } }
			non_aggression = { country = ENG country = U04 } 
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		control = { province = 1797 data = -3 } #Jerusalem in friendly hands
	}
	trigger = {
		event = 2002008
		war = { country = GER }
		war = { country = VIC }
		OR = {
			NOT = { war = { country = U04 } }
			non_aggression = { country = ENG country = U04 } 
		}
		control = { province = 1797 data = -3 }
		OR = {
			access = { country = U04 country = GER }
			access = { country = U04 country = ITA }
			event = { id = 2002008 days = 720 }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { control = { province = 1865 data = -2 } } #Baghdad allied or neutral
			event = { id = 2074001 days = 90 } #failed to suppress the Iraqi coup, attack anyway
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }
	
	name = "The Levant Campaign"
	desc = "On 28 May 1941, Admiral Darlan, on behalf of Vichy France, signed the Paris Protocols, an agreement which granted Germany access to military facilities in Vichy-controlled Syria. The protocols remained unratified, but Charles Huntziger, the Vichy Minister of War, sent orders to allow German and Italian aircraft to refuel in Syria. Marked as Iraqi aircraft, Axis aircraft landed in Syria en route to the Kingdom of Iraq during the Anglo-Iraqi War. This gave enough pretext for Britain to finally wrestle the Levant from the French and secure it for the Allies."
	picture = "Petain"
	style = 2
	
	action = {
		name = "Punish them!"
		command = { type = end_non_aggression which = U04 where = U01 }

		NOT = { capital = { province = 56 } } #Paris is not the capital of Free France
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
	      	control = { province = 56 } #Free France controls Paris
	      	AND = {
				control = { province = 949 } #Free France controls Algiers
				NOT = { capital = { province = 949 } }
				NOT = { control = { province = 56 } control = { province = 360 } } #Free France does not controls Paris
			}
		} 
	}
	trigger = {
		NOT = { capital = { province = 56 } } #Paris is not the capital of Free France
		OR = {
	      	control = { province = 56 } #Free France controls Paris
	      	AND = {
				control = { province = 949 } #Free France controls Algiers
				NOT = { capital = { province = 949 } }
				NOT = { control = { province = 56 } control = { province = 360 } } #Free France does not controls Paris or Vichy
			}
		} 
	}

	date = { day = 2 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

The so-called 'Plan D' envisioned approximately 20 French and 10 British divisions entering Belgium and setting up defenses along the Dyle river. This was partly intended to prevent the German forces from reaching French soil, particularly key industrial areas in the north, but the plan also had a political dimension. Failure to render assistance to the Belgian (and possibly Dutch) forces resisting a German invasion would be seen as cowardice and betrayal, and it would likely result either to the rapid destruction or rapid surrender of their forces, and a political fallout at home. event = {
	id = 2002071
	random = no
	country = SOV
	style = 2

	name = "Normandie-Niemen"
	desc = "When Operation Barbarossa began, the Soviet authorities declared their representatives of Vichy France persona non grata and asked them to return to France. Colonel Charles Luguet, the air attache of the Vichy government in Moscow, changed his allegiance to Free French. De Gaulle, believing in the importance of French soldiers serving on all fronts of the war, decided to engage forces on the Eastern Front in 1942. He initially proposed to send a mechanized division (the future 1st Free French Division, under General Edgard de Larminat) to the Eastern front. British opposition and the advice of Free French Air Forces commander Martial Henri Valin, however, made him opt for an air unit instead. Negotiations were lengthy, and Colonel Pougachev (military chief of the mission in London) opposed a separate French group near the Red Army."

	action = {
		name = "Merci!"
		command = { type = wakeleader which = 30819 } #Joseph Pouliquen SOV
		command = { type = add_corps which = "French Expeditionary Force" value = air where = 167 when = 30819 } #ivanovo
		command = { type = activate_division which = 10848 value = 8610 where = 167 when = 0 }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 2002072
	random = no
	tag = { U01 VIC }
	style = 2

	trigger = {
		event = 2002070
		OR = {
			war = { country = SOV }
			NOT = { war = { country = GER } }
			NOT = { war = { country = GER country = SOV } }
		}
	}

	date = { year = 1936 month = december day = 12 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { year = 1956 month = december day = 13 }

	name = "Normandie-Niemen returns home"
	desc = "Immediately after the surrender of Nazi Germany, Joseph Stalin decreed that combatants of Normandie-Niemen could return home together with their arms and aircraft. The squadron flew to Posen on 15 June, and to Prague the following day. They were received by General de Lattre de Tassigny in Stuttgart on 17 June. On 20 June, the squadron arrived at Saint-Dizier three days later. They were welcomed at Paris–Le Bourget Airport, and their 38 Yak-3s paraded down the Champs-Elysees."

	action = {
		name = "Welcome back"
		command = { type = remove_division which = 10848 value = 8610 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "E. de ch. 1/30 Normandie-Niemen" value = interceptor when = 9 where = -20 }
		command = { type = relation which = SOV value = 30 }
		command = { type = sleepleader which = 30819 } #Joseph Pouliquen SOV
		command = { type = wakeleader which = 5208 } #Joseph Pouliquen VIC
	}
}
